{"etymology_number":"1","lang_code":"en","pos":"conj","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"I got an earlier train to work so I'd have plenty of time to prepare for the meeting.","type":"example"},{"text":"Eat your broccoli so you can have dessert.","type":"example"}],"form_of":[{"word":"'so that'"}],"glosses":["Reduced form of 'so that', used to express purpose; in order that."],"tags":["form-of","reduced"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I was hungry, so I asked if there was any more food.","type":"example"},{"text":"He ate too much cake and so he fell ill.","type":"example"},{"text":"He wanted a book, so he went to the library.","type":"example"},{"text":"“I need to go to the bathroom.” ― “So go!”","type":"example"}],"glosses":["As a result; for that reason; therefore; because of this; due to this."]},{"examples":[{"text":"So how does this story end?","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used to connect previous conversation or events to the following question."]},{"examples":[{"text":"“We'd like to visit but I don't know if we can afford a hotel.” — “So who's staying in a hotel? Stay with us.”","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used to introduce a rhetorical question."]},{"glosses":["Provided that; on condition that; as long as."],"tags":["archaic"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"sō","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/səʊ/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/siː/","tags":["Northumbria"]},{"ipa":"/sə/","tags":["unstressed"]},{"enpr":"sō","tags":["US"]},{"ipa":"/soʊ/","tags":["US"]},{"ipa":"[soː]","tags":["Canada"]},{"ipa":"/so/","tags":["Scotland"]},{"ipa":"/soː/","tags":["Wales"]},{"ipa":"/sʌo/"}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"in order that","word":"so that"},{"sense":"in order that","word":"that"}],"upstream_line":22858,"upstream_offset":155826973,"upstream_record_sha256":"b5bc54b9167316fc71eec34ad2d848422a687d42cdfb6297761a2a71f7f6e139","word":"so"}
{"etymology_number":"1","lang_code":"en","pos":"adv","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"It was so hot outside that all the plants died.","type":"example"},{"text":"He was so good, they hired him on the spot.","type":"example"},{"text":"You behaviour so incensed me that I even thought of firing you.","type":"example"},{"text":"It was so cold a day that I could hardly breathe outside.","type":"example"},{"text":"So ridiculously did I do in the exam that mom grounded me for a month.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To the (explicitly stated) extent."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I need a piece of cloth so long. [= this long]","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To the (implied) extent."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I feel so much better now.","type":"example"},{"text":"I so nearly lost my temper.","type":"example"},{"text":"It’s not so bad. [i.e. it's acceptable]","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Very (positive or negative clause)."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"But I so want to see the Queen when she visits our town!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Very much."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"That is so not true!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Very much.","at all (negative clause)."],"tags":["informal","not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"As above, so below.","type":"example"},{"text":"Just as you have the right to your free speech, so I have the right to mine.","type":"example"},{"text":"Many people say she's the world's greatest athlete, but I don't think so.","type":"example"},{"text":"\"I can count backwards from one hundred.\" \"So can I.\"","type":"example"},{"text":"He wants to eat now. {"etymology_number":"1","lang_code":"en","pos":"intj","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"So, let's go home.","type":"example"},{"text":"So, what'll you have?","type":"example"},{"text":"So, there was this squirrel stuck in the chimney...","type":"example"},{"text":"So, everyone wants to know – did you win the contest or not?","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used after a pause for thought to introduce a new topic, question or story, or a new thought or question in continuation of an existing topic."],"synonyms":[{"word":"look"},{"word":"well"},{"word":"see"},{"word":"hey"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"\"You park your car in front of my house every morning.\" — \"So?\"","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used as a question to ask for further explanation of something said, often rhetorically or in a dismissive or impolite manner."]},{"examples":[{"text":"What are you doing? / So I'm just fixing this shelf.","type":"example"},{"text":"What time does the train leave? / So it leaves at 10 o'clock.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used as a meaningless filler word to begin a response to a question."]},{"glosses":["Be as you are; stand still; used especially to cows; also used by sailors."],"tags":["archaic"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"sō","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/səʊ/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/siː/","tags":["Northumbria"]},{"ipa":"/sə/","tags":["unstressed"]},{"enpr":"sō","tags":["US"]},{"ipa":"/soʊ/","tags":["US"]},{"ipa":"[soː]","tags":["Canada"]},{"ipa":"/so/","tags":["Scotland"]},{"ipa":"/soː/","tags":["Wales"]},{"ipa":"/sʌo/"}],"upstream_line":22861,"upstream_offset":155908808,"upstream_record_sha256":"fb0d7dc16746087793ce9b6ae6d6b5a41446a6aa4da219f45b1af1f04f6eacc7","word":"so"}
